function animateSenkuChemicalReaction(unitName,from,target,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const meta=canonicalUnit('senku').abilities.jutsu.presentation||{};
 const castDuration=meta.cast_duration_ms||680;
 const flightDuration=meta.flight_duration_ms||420;
 const impactDuration=meta.impact_duration_ms||620;

 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'special',start:performance.now(),duration:castDuration};

 if(meta.screen_dim_alpha){
   S.jutsuDim={start:performance.now(),alpha:meta.screen_dim_alpha,end:null};
 }

 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

   const projectile={
     kind:'senkuChemProjectile',
     from:{x:from.x,y:from.y-30},
     to:{x:target.x,y:target.y-10},
     start:performance.now(),
     duration:flightDuration,
     life:1
   };
   S.floaters.push(projectile);

   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     const impact={
       kind:'senkuChemImpact',
       x:target.x,
       y:target.y,
       start:performance.now(),
       duration:impactDuration,
       life:1
     };
     S.floaters.push(impact);

     try{onImpact&&onImpact()}
     catch(err){
       console.error('Senku Chemical Reaction impact failed:',err);
       return recoverAction('Senku Chemical Reaction impact');
     }

     setTimeout(()=>{
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==impact);
       if(S.jutsuDim)S.jutsuDim.end=performance.now();
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

       if(actionTokenAlive(token)){
         try{onDone&&onDone()}
         catch(err){recoverAction('Senku Chemical Reaction completion')}
       }
     },impactDuration);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.70);
}

function animateSenkuBomb(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone,attackKind='punch'){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const meta=canonicalUnit('senku')?.abilities?.basic?.presentation||{};
 const castDuration=meta.cast_duration_ms||560;
 const flightDuration=meta.flight_duration_ms||620;
 const blastDuration=meta.impact_duration_ms||430;
 const arcHeight=meta.arc_height_px||86;

 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 state.attackPose[unitName]={
   kind:attackKind,
   start:performance.now(),
   duration:castDuration
 };

 // Release near the final throwing pose rather than halfway through the cast.
 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

   const projectile={
     kind:'senkuBombProjectile',
     from:{x:from.x,y:from.y-31},
     to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-12},
     start:performance.now(),
     duration:flightDuration,
     arcHeight,
     life:1
   };
   S.floaters.push(projectile);

   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     const targetGroundY=(enemy.feetY??enemy.groundY??enemy.y);
     const blast={
       kind:'senkuExplosion',
       x:enemy.x,
       y:targetGroundY,
       layer:'ground',
       start:performance.now(),
       duration:360,
       life:1
     };
     S.floaters.push(blast);

     // Damage and target recoil happen only when the bomb lands.
     try{
       onImpact&&onImpact();
     }catch(err){
       console.error('Senku bomb impact failed:',err);
       return recoverAction('Senku bomb impact');
     }

     setTimeout(()=>{
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==blast);
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

       if(actionTokenAlive(token)){
         try{
           onDone&&onDone();
         }catch(err){
           recoverAction('Senku bomb completion');
         }
       }
     },blastDuration);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.76);
}

function setMeteorFullscreenFlash(color,opacity=1,transition='none'){
 const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
 if(!el)return;
 el.classList.add('active');
 el.style.transition=transition;
 el.style.background=color;
 el.style.opacity=String(opacity);
}

function clearMeteorFullscreenFlash(){
 const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
 if(!el)return;
 el.style.transition='none';
 el.style.opacity='0';
 el.style.background='transparent';
 el.classList.remove('active');
}

function playLebeeMeteorImpactFlash(done){
 const meta=canonicalUnit('lebee').abilities.jutsu.presentation||{};
 const whiteHold=meta.final_flash_white_hold_ms||170;
 const whiteFade=meta.final_flash_white_fade_ms||620;

 // Final meteor: snap to full white.
 setMeteorFullscreenFlash('#ffffff',1,'none');

 setTimeout(()=>{
   const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
   if(!el){
     done&&done();
     return;
   }
   el.style.transition=`opacity ${whiteFade}ms ease-out`;
   el.style.opacity='0';

   setTimeout(()=>{
     clearMeteorFullscreenFlash();
     done&&done();
   },whiteFade+20);
 },whiteHold);
}

function finishAction(){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 clearActionWatchdog();
 setTimeout(()=>{
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  // v0.5.10: retire the completed action token BEFORE clearing S.anim.
  // This prevents late recoil/requestAnimationFrame callbacks from recreating
  // S.anim after the turn has returned to charge, which caused the hard freeze.
  invalidateActions();
  S.anim=null;S.ready=null;S.action='normal';
  if(S.victoryFX||!aliveEnemies().length){
   if(!S.victoryFX)startVictorySequence();
   S.phase='resolve';
   return;
  }
  if(!alivePairs().length){
   S.phase='resolve';
   S.log='Defeat — all three fighter pairs have been knocked out.';
   return;
  }
  S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 },220)
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.
 for(let pass=0;pass<8;pass++){
   let blocker=null;
   let blockerDist=Infinity;

   S.pairs.forEach(pair=>{
     if(!pairAlive(pair))return;
     let dist=d(enemy,pair);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(other=>{
     if(other===enemy||other.hp<=0)return;
     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(!blocker)break;

   let dx=enemy.x-blocker.x;
   let dy=enemy.y-blocker.y;
   let len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);

   // Exact same-center fallback gets a deterministic escape direction.
   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;
   }

   let push=(minDist-len)+.75;
   enemy.x+=(dx/len)*push;
   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=clampToBattlefield(enemy);
   enemy.x=legal.x;
   enemy.y=legal.y;
 }
}

function cpuTurn(){
 if(S.phase!=='cpu')return;
 const cpuActionToken=beginActionToken();

 // Weighted considerations:
 // - nearest target
 // - lowest-health target

 function nearbyCount(p){
   return candidates.filter(other=>other!==p && d(p,other)<145).length;
 }

       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==impact);
       if(S.jutsuDim)S.jutsuDim.end=performance.now();
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

       if(actionTokenAlive(token)){
         try{onDone&&onDone()}
         catch(err){recoverAction('Senku Chemical Reaction completion')}
       }
     },impactDuration);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.70);
}

function animateSenkuBomb(unitName,from,enemy,onImpact,onDone,attackKind='punch'){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const meta=canonicalUnit('senku')?.abilities?.basic?.presentation||{};
 const castDuration=meta.cast_duration_ms||560;
 const flightDuration=meta.flight_duration_ms||620;
 const blastDuration=meta.impact_duration_ms||430;
 const arcHeight=meta.arc_height_px||86;

 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};
 state.attackPose[unitName]={
   kind:attackKind,
   start:performance.now(),
   duration:castDuration
 };

 // Release near the final throwing pose rather than halfway through the cast.
 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

   const projectile={
     kind:'senkuBombProjectile',
     from:{x:from.x,y:from.y-31},
     to:{x:enemy.x,y:enemy.y-12},
     start:performance.now(),
     duration:flightDuration,
     arcHeight,
     life:1
   };
   S.floaters.push(projectile);

   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

     S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==projectile);
     const targetGroundY=(enemy.feetY??enemy.groundY??enemy.y);
     const blast={
       kind:'senkuExplosion',
       x:enemy.x,
       y:targetGroundY,
       layer:'ground',
       start:performance.now(),
       duration:360,
       life:1
     };
     S.floaters.push(blast);

     // Damage and target recoil happen only when the bomb lands.
     try{
       onImpact&&onImpact();
     }catch(err){
       console.error('Senku bomb impact failed:',err);
       return recoverAction('Senku bomb impact');
     }

     setTimeout(()=>{
       S.floaters=S.floaters.filter(x=>x!==blast);
       const st=ensureAnimState();
       if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

       if(actionTokenAlive(token)){
         try{
           onDone&&onDone();
         }catch(err){
           recoverAction('Senku bomb completion');
         }
       }
     },blastDuration);
   },flightDuration);
 },castDuration*.76);
}

function setMeteorFullscreenFlash(color,opacity=1,transition='none'){
 const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
 if(!el)return;
 el.classList.add('active');
 el.style.transition=transition;
 el.style.background=color;
 el.style.opacity=String(opacity);
}

function clearMeteorFullscreenFlash(){
 const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
 if(!el)return;
 el.style.transition='none';
 el.style.opacity='0';
 el.style.background='transparent';
 el.classList.remove('active');
}

function playLebeeMeteorImpactFlash(done){
 const meta=canonicalUnit('lebee').abilities.jutsu.presentation||{};
 const whiteHold=meta.final_flash_white_hold_ms||170;
 const whiteFade=meta.final_flash_white_fade_ms||620;

 // Final meteor: snap to full white.
 setMeteorFullscreenFlash('#ffffff',1,'none');

 setTimeout(()=>{
   const el=getMeteorScreenFlash();
   if(!el){
     done&&done();
     return;
   }
   el.style.transition=`opacity ${whiteFade}ms ease-out`;
   el.style.opacity='0';

   setTimeout(()=>{
     clearMeteorFullscreenFlash();
     done&&done();
   },whiteFade+20);
 },whiteHold);
}

function animateLebeeMeteorJutsu(unitName,from,targets,onImpact,onDone){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 const meta=canonicalUnit('lebee').abilities.jutsu.presentation||{};
 const castDuration=meta.cast_duration_ms||900;
 const fallDuration=meta.meteor_fall_duration_ms||700;
 const meteorCount=meta.meteor_count||7;
 const stagger=meta.meteor_stagger_ms||72;
 const spreadX=meta.impact_scatter_x||105;
 const spreadY=meta.impact_scatter_y||62;
 const yellowMs=meta.final_flash_yellow_ms||190;

 const state=ensureAnimState();
 if(!state.attackPose)state.attackPose={};

 clearMeteorFullscreenFlash();

 // Cast / summon / spin sequence.
 state.attackPose[unitName]={kind:'special',start:performance.now(),duration:castDuration};
 S.jutsuDim={start:performance.now(),alpha:meta.screen_dim_alpha||.34,end:null};

 setTimeout(()=>{
   if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;

   const st=ensureAnimState();
   if(st.attackPose)delete st.attackPose[unitName];

   const livingTargets=targets.filter(e=>e.hp>0);
   const fallback={x:W/2,y:H*.34};
   const targetPoints=livingTargets.length?livingTargets:[fallback];
   const meteors=[];
   const golden=2.399963229728653;

   const casterOnLeft=from.x < W/2;
   const entryCenterX=casterOnLeft ? W*.78 : W*.22;
   const entrySpread=meta.meteor_entry_horizontal_spread||92;

   for(let i=0;i<meteorCount;i++){
     const target=targetPoints[i%targetPoints.length];

     // Tight impact spread around the target.
     const ring=.28+.72*Math.sqrt((i+1)/meteorCount);
     const ang=i*golden+.65;
     const landX=clamp(target.x+Math.cos(ang)*spreadX*ring,BATTLE_BOUNDS.left+24,BATTLE_BOUNDS.right-24);
     const landY=clamp(target.y+Math.sin(ang)*spreadY*ring,BATTLE_BOUNDS.top+28,BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom-28);

     // Enter from the upper side opposite Lebee, grouped rather than screen-wide.
     const entryOffset=((i/Math.max(1,meteorCount-1))-.5)*

===== validTargets =====

--- match 1 @ 43605306 ---
 slot.
 let angles=[];
 for(let i=0;i<10;i++)angles.push((Math.PI*2/10)*i);

 let best=null;
 angles.forEach(a=>{
   let radius=58+Math.random()*35;
   let pos=clampToBattlefield({
     x:target.x+Math.cos(a)*radius,
     y:target.y+Math.sin(a)*radius
   });

   // Score position by direct hits + mutual proximity links with enemy allies.
   let testEnemy={...e,x:pos.x,y:pos.y};
   testEnemy.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-pos.y,target.x-pos.x);
   let hitCount=0;
   let comboSupport=0;

   candidates.forEach(p=>{
     let body={x:p.x,y:p.y,r:18};
     let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0};
     if(hits(testEnemy,fakeUnit,body))hitCount++;
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(ally=>{
     if(ally===e||ally.hp<=0)return;

     let bodySelf={x:testEnemy.x,y:testEnemy.y,r:testEnemy.r||16};
     let bodyAlly={x:ally.x,y:ally.y,r:ally.r||16};

     let selfCoversAlly=hits(testEnemy,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0},bodyAlly);
     let allyCoversSelf=hits(ally,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:ally.rotation||0},bodySelf);

     if(selfCoversAlly&&allyCoversSelf)comboSupport++;
   });

   let movement=d(e,pos);
   let targetDistance=d(pos,target);
   let score=
     hitCount*92 +
     comboSupport*38 -
     movement*.12 -
     targetDistance*.16 +
     Math.random()*10;

   if(!best || score>best.score)best={pos,score,hitCount,comboSupport};
 });

 let desired=best?.pos || clampToBattlefield({x:target.x-55,y:target.y});
 let from={x:e.x,y:e.y},start=performance.now(),dur=520;
 S.anim={positions:{}};

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);
     resolveEnemyOverlap(e);
     delete S.anim.positions[e.name];
     S.anim.showEnemyRange=e;
     setTimeout(()=>cpuAttack(e,target),360);
   }
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(move);
}
function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

 let from={x:e.x,y:e.y},start=performance.now(),dur=520;
 S.anim={positions:{}};

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);
     resolveEnemyOverlap(e);
     delete S.anim.positions[e.name];
     S.anim.showEnemyRange=e;
     setTimeout(()=>cpuAttack(e,target),360);
   }
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(move);
}
function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

20L36%2018%20L42%2025%27%20f

--- match 6 @ 43605892 ---
(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0};
     if(hits(testEnemy,fakeUnit,body))hitCount++;
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(ally=>{
     if(ally===e||ally.hp<=0)return;

     let bodySelf={x:testEnemy.x,y:testEnemy.y,r:testEnemy.r||16};
     let bodyAlly={x:ally.x,y:ally.y,r:ally.r||16};

     let selfCoversAlly=hits(testEnemy,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0},bodyAlly);
     let allyCoversSelf=hits(ally,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:ally.rotation||0},bodySelf);

     if(selfCoversAlly&&allyCoversSelf)comboSupport++;
   });

   let movement=d(e,pos);
   let targetDistance=d(pos,target);
   let score=
     hitCount*92 +
     comboSupport*38 -
     movement*.12 -
     targetDistance*.16 +
     Math.random()*10;

   if(!best || score>best.score)best={pos,score,hitCount,comboSupport};
 });

 let desired=best?.pos || clampToBattlefield({x:target.x-55,y:target.y});
 let from={x:e.x,y:e.y},start=performance.now(),dur=520;
 S.anim={positions:{}};

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);
     resolveEnemyOverlap(e);
     delete S.anim.positions[e.name];
     S.anim.showEnemyRange=e;
     setTimeout(()=>cpuAttack(e,target),360);
   }
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(move);
}
function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

 function move(n){

function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

function finishAction(){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 clearActionWatchdog();
 setTimeout(()=>{
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  // v0.5.10: retire the completed action token BEFORE clearing S.anim.
  // This prevents late recoil/requestAnimationFrame callbacks from recreating
  // S.anim after the turn has returned to charge, which caused the hard freeze.
  invalidateActions();
  S.anim=null;S.ready=null;S.action='normal';
  if(S.victoryFX||!aliveEnemies().length){
   if(!S.victoryFX)startVictorySequence();
   S.phase='resolve';
   return;
  }
  if(!alivePairs().length){
   S.phase='resolve';
   S.log='Defeat — all three fighter pairs have been knocked out.';
   return;
  }
  S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 },220)
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.
 for(let pass=0;pass<8;pass++){
   let blocker=null;
   let blockerDist=Infinity;

   S.pairs.forEach(pair=>{
     if(!pairAlive(pair))return;
     let dist=d(enemy,pair);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(other=>{
     if(other===enemy||other.hp<=0)return;
     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(!blocker)break;

   let dx=enemy.x-blocker.x;
   let dy=enemy.y-blocker.y;
   let len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);

   // Exact same-center fallback gets a deterministic escape direction.
   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;
   }

   let push=(minDist-len)+.75;
   enemy.x+=(dx/len)*push;
   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=clampToBattlefield(enemy);
   enemy.x=legal.x;
   enemy.y=legal.y;
 }
}

function finishAction(){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 clearActionWatchdog();
 setTimeout(()=>{
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  // v0.5.10: retire the completed action token BEFORE clearing S.anim.
  // This prevents late recoil/requestAnimationFrame callbacks from recreating
  // S.anim after the turn has returned to charge, which caused the hard freeze.
  invalidateActions();
  S.anim=null;S.ready=null;S.action='normal';
  if(S.victoryFX||!aliveEnemies().length){
   if(!S.victoryFX)startVictorySequence();
   S.phase='resolve';
   return;
  }
  if(!alivePairs().length){
   S.phase='resolve';
   S.log='Defeat — all three fighter pairs have been knocked out.';
   return;
  }
  S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 },220)
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.
 for(let pass=0;pass<8;pass++){
   let blocker=null;
   let blockerDist=Infinity;

   S.pairs.forEach(pair=>{
     if(!pairAlive(pair))return;
     let dist=d(enemy,pair);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(other=>{
     if(other===enemy||other.hp<=0)return;
     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(!blocker)break;

   let dx=enemy.x-blocker.x;
   let dy=enemy.y-blocker.y;
   let len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);

   // Exact same-center fallback gets a deterministic escape direction.
   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;
   }

   let push=(minDist-len)+.75;
   enemy.x+=(dx/len)*push;
   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=clampToBattlefield(enemy);
   enemy.x=legal.x;
   enemy.y=legal.y;
 }
}

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);

function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

     let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0};
     if(hits(testEnemy,fakeUnit,body))hitCount++;
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(ally=>{
     if(ally===e||ally.hp<=0)return;

     let bodySelf={x:testEnemy.x,y:testEnemy.y,r:testEnemy.r||16};
     let bodyAlly={x:ally.x,y:ally.y,r:ally.r||16};

     let selfCoversAlly=hits(testEnemy,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0},bodyAlly);
     let allyCoversSelf=hits(ally,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:ally.rotation||0},bodySelf);

     if(selfCoversAlly&&allyCoversSelf)comboSupport++;
   });

   let movement=d(e,pos);
   let targetDistance=d(pos,target);
   let score=
     hitCount*92 +
     comboSupport*38 -
     movement*.12 -
     targetDistance*.16 +
     Math.random()*10;

   if(!best || score>best.score)best={pos,score,hitCount,comboSupport};
 });

 let desired=best?.pos || clampToBattlefield({x:target.x-55,y:target.y});
 let from={x:e.x,y:e.y},start=performance.now(),dur=520;
 S.anim={positions:{}};

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);
     resolveEnemyOverlap(e);
     delete S.anim.positions[e.name];
     S.anim.showEnemyRange=e;
     setTimeout(()=>cpuAttack(e,target),360);
   }
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(move);
}
function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

function finishAction(){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 clearActionWatchdog();
 setTimeout(()=>{
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  // v0.5.10: retire the completed action token BEFORE clearing S.anim.
  // This prevents late recoil/requestAnimationFrame callbacks from recreating
  // S.anim after the turn has returned to charge, which caused the hard freeze.
  invalidateActions();
  S.anim=null;S.ready=null;S.action='normal';
  if(S.victoryFX||!aliveEnemies().length){
   if(!S.victoryFX)startVictorySequence();
   S.phase='resolve';
   return;
  }
  if(!alivePairs().length){
   S.phase='resolve';
   S.log='Defeat — all three fighter pairs have been knocked out.';
   return;
  }
  S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 },220)
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.
 for(let pass=0;pass<8;pass++){
   let blocker=null;
   let blockerDist=Infinity;

   S.pairs.forEach(pair=>{
     if(!pairAlive(pair))return;
     let dist=d(enemy,pair);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(other=>{
     if(other===enemy||other.hp<=0)return;
     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(!blocker)break;

   let dx=enemy.x-blocker.x;
   let dy=enemy.y-blocker.y;
   let len=Math.hypot(dx,dy);

   // Exact same-center fallback gets a deterministic escape direction.
   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;
   }

   let push=(minDist-len)+.75;
   enemy.x+=(dx/len)*push;
   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=clampToBattlefield(enemy);
   enemy.x=legal.x;
   enemy.y=legal.y;
 }
}

function finishAction(){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 clearActionWatchdog();
 setTimeout(()=>{
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  // v0.5.10: retire the completed action token BEFORE clearing S.anim.
  // This prevents late recoil/requestAnimationFrame callbacks from recreating
  // S.anim after the turn has returned to charge, which caused the hard freeze.
  invalidateActions();
  S.anim=null;S.ready=null;S.action='normal';
  if(S.victoryFX||!aliveEnemies().length){
   if(!S.victoryFX)startVictorySequence();
   S.phase='resolve';
   return;
  }
  if(!alivePairs().length){
   S.phase='resolve';
   S.log='Defeat — all three fighter pairs have been knocked out.';
   return;
  }
  S.phase='charge';S._chargeSince=performance.now();
 },220)
}

function enemiesMutuallyLinked(a,b){
 if(a===b || a.hp<=0 || b.hp<=0)return false;

 let bodyA={x:a.x,y:a.y,r:a.r||16};
 let bodyB={x:b.x,y:b.y,r:b.r||16};

 let aCoversB=hits(a,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(b,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:0},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}

function enemyComboMembers(targetPair,initiator){
 // Target is no longer part of combo eligibility.
 // Once linked, the helper may join the initiator's valid attack target.
 return S.enemies.filter(enemy=>{
   if(enemy.hp<=0)return false;
   return enemy===initiator || enemiesMutuallyLinked(initiator,enemy)
 })
}

function resolveEnemyOverlap(enemy){
 const minDist=47;

 // Resolve only the enemy that just moved. This intentionally does not
 // mutate player positions or other enemy positions.

     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=pair;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

     let dist=d(enemy,other);
     if(dist<minDist && dist<blockerDist){
       blocker=other;
       blockerDist=dist;
     }
   });

   if(len<.01){
     let angle=(pass+1)*1.913;
     dx=Math.cos(angle);
     dy=Math.sin(angle);
     len=1;

   enemy.y+=(dy/len)*push;

   let legal=clampToBattlefield(enemy);
   enemy.x=legal.x;
   enemy.y=legal.y;
 }
}

function cpuTurn(){
 if(S.phase!=='cpu')return;
 const cpuActionToken=beginActionToken();
 armActionWatchdog('Anubis / enemy turn',cpuActionToken);
 let e=S.ready.ref;
 if(e.hp<=0){e.gauge=0;return finishAction()}

 let candidates=S.pairs.filter(pairAlive);candidates.forEach(ensureFront);
 if(!candidates.length)return;

 // Enemy personality changes each turn so the CPU does not dogpile one unit.
 // Weighted considerations:
 // - nearest target
 // - lowest-health target
 // - clustered target opportunity
 // - mild randomness so equal situations do not look scripted
 const modes=['nearest','weakest','cluster','balanced'];
 const mode=modes[Math.floor(Math.random()*modes.length)];

 function nearbyCount(p){
   return candidates.filter(other=>other!==p && d(p,other)<145).length;
 }

 function targetScore(p){
   let u=front(p);
   let dist=d(e,p);
   let hpRatio=u.hp/u.maxHp;
   let cluster=nearbyCount(p);
   let jitter=Math.random()*12;

   if(mode==='nearest')
     return dist*1.35 + hpRatio*30 - cluster*8 + jitter;
   if(mode==='weakest')
     return hpRatio*150 + dist*.35 - cluster*6 + jitter;
   if(mode==='cluster')
     return dist*.45 + hpRatio*35 - cluster*55 + jitter;

   // balanced
   return dist*.7 + hpRatio*75 - cluster*24 + jitter;
 }

 let target=[...candidates].sort((a,b)=>targetScore(a)-targetScore(b))[0];

 // Evaluate several approach angles instead of always taking the same straight-line slot.
 let angles=[];
 for(let i=0;i<10;i++)angles.push((Math.PI*2/10)*i);

 let best=null;
 angles.forEach(a=>{
   let radius=58+Math.random()*35;
   let pos=clampToBattlefield({
     x:target.x+Math.cos(a)*radius,
     y:target.y+Ma

--- match 4 @ 43605712 ---
h.atan2(target.y-pos.y,target.x-pos.x);
   let hitCount=0;
   let comboSupport=0;

   candidates.forEach(p=>{
     let body={x:p.x,y:p.y,r:18};
     let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0};
     if(hits(testEnemy,fakeUnit,body))hitCount++;
   });

   S.enemies.forEach(ally=>{
     if(ally===e||ally.hp<=0)return;

     let bodySelf={x:testEnemy.x,y:testEnemy.y,r:testEnemy.r||16};
     let bodyAlly={x:ally.x,y:ally.y,r:ally.r||16};

     let selfCoversAlly=hits(testEnemy,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0},bodyAlly);
     let allyCoversSelf=hits(ally,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:ally.rotation||0},bodySelf);

     if(selfCoversAlly&&allyCoversSelf)comboSupport++;
   });

   let movement=d(e,pos);
   let targetDistance=d(pos,target);
   let score=
     hitCount*92 +
     comboSupport*38 -
     movement*.12 -
     targetDistance*.16 +
     Math.random()*10;

   if(!best || score>best.score)best={pos,score,hitCount,comboSupport};
 });

 let desired=best?.pos || clampToBattlefield({x:target.x-55,y:target.y});
 let from={x:e.x,y:e.y},start=performance.now(),dur=520;
 S.anim={positions:{}};

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);
     resolveEnemyOverlap(e);
     delete S.anim.positions[e.name];
     S.anim.showEnemyRange=e;
     setTimeout(()=>cpuAttack(e,target),360);
   }
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(move);
}
function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}




// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SUMMONS SIDE SYSTEM — dedicated full-screen pull sequence.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
let summonEmbers=9999;
let summonPulls=0;
let summonSequenceToken=0;

const COMMON_SUMMON_SPRITES={'Red Ninja':'data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%27http%3A//www.w3.org/2000/svg%27%20width%3D%2772%27%20height%3D%2796%27%20viewBox%3D%270%200%2072%2096%27%3E%0A%20%20%3Cdefs%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%3ClinearGradient%20id%3D%27bg%27%20x1%3D%270%27%20y1%3D%270%27%20x2%3D%270%27%20y2%3D%271%27%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%20%20%3Cstop%20offset%3D%270%25%27%20stop-color%3D%27%23202833%27/%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%20%20%3Cstop%20offset%3D%27100%25%27%20stop-color%3D%27%230e141a%27/%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%3C/linearGradient%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%3ClinearGradient%20id%3D%27robe%27%20x1%3D%270%27%20y1%3D%270%27%20x2%3D%271%27%20y2%3D%271%27%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%20%20%3Cstop%20offset%3D%270%25%27%20stop-color%3D%27%23e13a42%27/%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%20%20%3Cstop%20offset%3D%27100%25%27%20stop-color%3D%27%237b1118%27/%3E%0A%20%20%20%20%3C/linearGradient%3E%0A%20%20%3C/defs%3E%0A%20%20%3Crect%20x%3D%270%27%20y%3D%270%27%20width%3D%2772%27%20height%3D%2796%27%20rx%3D%2710%27%20fill%3D%27url%28%23bg%29%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Cellipse%20cx%3D%2736%27%20cy%3D%2790%27%20rx%3D%2718%27%20ry%3D%274%27%20fill%3D%27rgba%280%2C0%2C0%2C.28%29%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%27M19%2078%20L27%2046%20L45%2046%20L53%2078%20Z%27%20fill%3D%27url%28%23robe%29%27%20stroke%3D%27%23111%27%20stroke-width%3D%271.2%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%27M22%2046%20C25%2029%2C%2047%2029%2C%2050%2046%20L46%2060%20L26%2060%20Z%27%20fill%3D%27%2315181d%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%27M24%2045%20C25%2027%2C%2047%2027%2C%2048%2045%20L44%2055%20L28%2055%20Z%27%20fill%3D%27url%28%23robe%29%27%20stroke%3D%27%23111%27%20stroke-width%3D%271.2%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Crect%20x%3D%2725%27%20y%3D%2741%27%20width%3D%2722%27%20height%3D%278%27%20rx%3D%274%27%20fill%3D%27%230a0c10%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Crect%20x%3D%2728%27%20y%3D%2743%27%20width%3D%276%27%20height%3D%272.6%27%20rx%3D%271.2%27%20fill%3D%27%23f3e7c1%27/%3E%0A%20%20%3Crect%20x%3D%2738%27%20y%3D%2

--- match 5 @ 43606132 ---
yAlly={x:ally.x,y:ally.y,r:ally.r||16};

     let selfCoversAlly=hits(testEnemy,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:testEnemy.rotation||0},bodyAlly);
     let allyCoversSelf=hits(ally,{shape:normalShape(),rotation:ally.rotation||0},bodySelf);

     if(selfCoversAlly&&allyCoversSelf)comboSupport++;
   });

   let movement=d(e,pos);
   let targetDistance=d(pos,target);
   let score=
     hitCount*92 +
     comboSupport*38 -
     movement*.12 -
     targetDistance*.16 +
     Math.random()*10;

   if(!best || score>best.score)best={pos,score,hitCount,comboSupport};
 });

 let desired=best?.pos || clampToBattlefield({x:target.x-55,y:target.y});
 let from={x:e.x,y:e.y},start=performance.now(),dur=520;
 S.anim={positions:{}};

 S.log=`${e.name} is evaluating a ${mode} attack.`;

 function move(n){
   let t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/dur),q=t*t*(3-2*t);
   S.anim.positions[e.name]={
     x:from.x+(desired.x-from.x)*q,
     y:from.y+(desired.y-from.y)*q
   };

   if(t<1)requestAnimationFrame(move);
   else{
     let legal=clampToBattlefield(desired);
     e.x=legal.x;e.y=legal.y;
     e.rotation=Math.atan2(target.y-e.y,target.x-e.x);
     resolveEnemyOverlap(e);
     delete S.anim.positions[e.name];
     S.anim.showEnemyRange=e;
     setTimeout(()=>cpuAttack(e,target),360);
   }
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(move);
}
function cpuAttack(e,p){
 S.anim.showEnemyRange=null;

 let validTargets=S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   let body={x:pair.x,y:pair.y,r:18};
   let fakeUnit={shape:normalShape(),rotation:e.rotation||0};
   return hits(e,fakeUnit,body)
 });

 // Safety fallback if movement somehow ends just outside its chosen target.
 if(!validTargets.length)validTargets=[p];

 let targetIndex=0;
 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

 let anyCombo=false;

 function runTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=validTargets.length){
     e.gauge=0;
     S.log=anyCombo
       ? `${e.name}'s side completed a combo attack across ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`
       : `${e.name} hit ${validTargets.length} target${validTargets.length===1?'':'s'}.`;
     return finishAction()
   }

   let targetPair=validTargets[targetIndex++];
   if(!pairAlive(targetPair))return runTarget();
   ensureFront(targetPair);

   // Initiator first, then every other enemy whose current normal range
   // covers this same player target.
   let helpers=enemyComboMembers(targetPair,e).filter(enemy=>enemy!==e);
   if(helpers.length)anyCombo=true;
   let attackers=[e,...helpers];
   let attackerIndex=0;

   function runAttacker(){
     if(!pairAlive(targetPair) || attackerIndex>=attackers.length)
       return setTimeout(runTarget,100);

     ensureFront(targetPair);
     if(front(targetPair).hp<=0)return setTimeout(runTarget,100);
     let victim=front(targetPair);
     let attacker=attackers[attackerIndex++];

     let from={x:attacker.x,y:attacker.y},
         dx=targetPair.x-attacker.x,
         dy=targetPair.y-attacker.y,
         len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy)),
         to={
           x:targetPair.x-dx/len*42,
           y:targetPair.y-dy/len*42
         };

     animateLunge(
       attacker.name,from,to,
       ()=>{
         victim.hp=Math.max(0,victim.hp-attacker.attack);
         addImpactFlash(targetPair.x,targetPair.y-8,'#ff8b8b');
         addFloat(
           targetPair.x,
           targetPair.y-35,
           '-'+attacker.attack,
           attacker===e?'#ff8b8b':'#ffbd4a'
         );

         let pDir=Math.sign(targetPair.x-attacker.x)||1;
         startHitReaction(victim.name,pDir,attackerIndex>1);

         if(victim.hp<=0){
           handleKO(targetPair,victim.name,pDir);
         }
       },
       ()=>setTimeout(runAttacker,85),
       attackerIndex%2===1?'punch':'kick'
     )
   }

   runAttacker()
 }

 runTarget()
}

function unitsMutuallyLinked(pairA,pairB){
 if(pairA===pairB || !pairAlive(pairA) || !pairAlive(pairB))return false;
 ensureFront(pairA);ensureFront(pairB);

 let unitA=front(pairA),unitB=front(pairB);
 let bodyA={x:pairA.x,y:pairA.y,r:16};
 let bodyB={x:pairB.x,y:pairB.y,r:16};

 // Always use NORMAL attack geometry for combo-link eligibility.
 let aCoversB=hits(pairA,{...unitA,shape:normalShape()},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(pairB,{...unitB,shape:normalShape()},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}


function linkBuffFromProvider(providerPair,receiverPair){
 if(!pairAlive(providerPair)||!pairAlive(receiverPair)||providerPair===receiverPair)
   return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 ensureFront(providerPair);ensureFront(receiverPair);
 let provider=front(providerPair);
 if(!provider.linkBuff)return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 // Buff only matters when the two units are currently combo-linked.
 if(!unitsMutuallyLinked(providerPair,receiverPair))
   return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 let dist=d(providerPair,receiverPair);
 let range=provider.linkBuff.range;
 if(dist>range)return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 // A small "full power" distance keeps the curve from exploding at zero.
 let inner=34;
 let t=1-clamp((dist-inner)/Math.max(1,range-inner),0,1);
 let bonus=provider.linkBuff.minBonus+
           (provider.linkBuff.maxBonus-provider.linkBuff.minBonus)*t;

 return {bonus,ratio:t,provider};
}

function totalLinkAttackBuff(receiverPair){
 let total=0,bestRatio=0,providers=[];
 S.pairs.forEach(providerPair=>{
   if(providerPair===receiverPair)return;
   let result=linkBuffFromProvider(providerPair,receiverPair);
   if(result.bonus>0){
     total+=result.bonus;
     bestRatio=Math.max(bestRatio,result.ratio);
     providers.push(result.provider.name);
   }
 });
 return {bonus:total,ratio:bestRatio,providers};
}

function buffedNormalDamage(pair){
 let u=front(pair);
 let buff=totalLinkAttackBuff(pair);
 return {
   base:u.attack,
   buff,
   damage:Math.round(u.attack*(1+buff.bonus))
 };
}

function comboMembers(enemy,initiatorPair){
 // Enemy argument is retained because the attack sequence calls this per target,
 // but combo membership itself is determined only by ally-to-ally range linking.
 if(!pairAlive(initiatorPair))return [];
 ensureFront(initiatorPair);

function unitsMutuallyLinked(pairA,pairB){
 if(pairA===pairB || !pairAlive(pairA) || !pairAlive(pairB))return false;
 ensureFront(pairA);ensureFront(pairB);

 let unitA=front(pairA),unitB=front(pairB);
 let bodyA={x:pairA.x,y:pairA.y,r:16};
 let bodyB={x:pairB.x,y:pairB.y,r:16};

 // Always use NORMAL attack geometry for combo-link eligibility.
 let aCoversB=hits(pairA,{...unitA,shape:normalShape()},bodyB);
 let bCoversA=hits(pairB,{...unitB,shape:normalShape()},bodyA);

 return aCoversB && bCoversA
}


function linkBuffFromProvider(providerPair,receiverPair){
 if(!pairAlive(providerPair)||!pairAlive(receiverPair)||providerPair===receiverPair)
   return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 ensureFront(providerPair);ensureFront(receiverPair);
 let provider=front(providerPair);
 if(!provider.linkBuff)return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 // Buff only matters when the two units are currently combo-linked.
 if(!unitsMutuallyLinked(providerPair,receiverPair))
   return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 let dist=d(providerPair,receiverPair);
 let range=provider.linkBuff.range;
 if(dist>range)return {bonus:0,ratio:0,provider:null};

 // A small "full power" distance keeps the curve from exploding at zero.
 let inner=34;
 let t=1-clamp((dist-inner)/Math.max(1,range-inner),0,1);
 let bonus=provider.linkBuff.minBonus+
           (provider.linkBuff.maxBonus-provider.linkBuff.minBonus)*t;

 return {bonus,ratio:t,provider};
}

function totalLinkAttackBuff(receiverPair){
 let total=0,bestRatio=0,providers=[];
 S.pairs.forEach(providerPair=>{
   if(providerPair===receiverPair)return;
   let result=linkBuffFromProvider(providerPair,receiverPair);
   if(result.bonus>0){
     total+=result.bonus;
     bestRatio=Math.max(bestRatio,result.ratio);
     providers.push(result.provider.name);
   }
 });
 return {bonus:total,ratio:bestRatio,providers};
}

function buffedNormalDamage(pair){
 let u=front(pair);
 let buff=totalLinkAttackBuff(pair);
 return {
   base:u.attack,
   buff,
   damage:Math.round(u.attack*(1+buff.bonus))
 };
}

function comboMembers(enemy,initiatorPair){
 // Enemy argument is retained because the attack sequence calls this per target,
 // but combo membership itself is determined only by ally-to-ally range linking.
 if(!pairAlive(initiatorPair))return [];
 ensureFront(initiatorPair);

 return S.pairs.filter(pair=>{
   if(!pairAlive(pair))return false;
   ensureFront(pair);
   return pair===initiatorPair || unitsMutuallyLinked(initiatorPair,pair)
 })
}

  delete state.positions[enemy.name];

}

// Ability-tier lookup must live at battle scope because both the ground-field

function isUltimateAction(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   // canonicalUnit already resolves both IDs and display names through UNIT_NAME_TO_ID.
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return !!(ability?.ultimate===true || ability?.category==='ultimate' || ability?.tier==='ultimate');
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}

function isAllySupportJutsu(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return ability?.target==='all_allies' || ability?.effect==='heal_full';
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}


function ensureFront(p){

 if(front(p).hp<=0 && koAnimating(front(p).name))return true;
 if(back(p).hp>0){p.active=1-p.active;return true}
 return false
}

function local(p,u,e){let a=-(u.rotation||0),dx=e.x-p.x,dy=e.y-p.y;return{x:dx*Math.cos(a)-dy*Math.sin(a),y:dx*Math.sin(a)+dy*Math.cos(a)}}

function hits(p,u,e){let s=u.shape,q=local(p,u,e),er=e.r||20;

 if(s.type==='rect'){
   const rq={x:q.x-(s.offset_x||0),y:q.y-(s.offset_y||0)};
   return circleRect(rq,er,s.w/2,s.h/2);
 }
 if(s.type==='square')return circleRect(q,er,s.s/2,s.s/2);
 if(s.type==='cross')return circleRect(q,er,s.l/2,s.t/2)||circleRect(q,er,s.t/2,s.l/2);
 if(s.type==='donut'){let z=Math.hypot(q.x,q.y);return z+er>=s.i&&z-er<=s.o}
 if(s.type==='cone'){let z=Math.hypot(q.x,q.y),ang=Math.abs(Math.atan2(q.y,q.x)),pad=Math.asin(Math.min(1,er/Math.max(z,.01)));return q.x+er>=0&&z-er<=s.r&&ang<=s.a/2+pad}
 return false}

     w=h*ratio;
   }

   ctx.save();
   ctx.globalAlpha=textEase;
   ctx.translate(W/2,H/2-8);
   ctx.scale(scale,scale);
   ctx.shadowColor=`rgba(225,0,0,${.20*textEase})`;
   ctx.shadowBlur=13*textEase;
   ctx.drawImage(VICTORY_IMAGE,-w/2,-h/2,w,h);
   ctx.restore();
 }else{
   // Edge/iOS fallback: victory is never invisible because an image decode lagged.
   ctx.save();
   ctx.globalAlpha=textEase;
   ctx.translate(W/2,H/2-8);
   ctx.scale(scale,scale);
   ctx.textAlign='center';
   ctx.textBaseline='middle';
   ctx.font='64px BambooBrush, Impact, system-ui';
   ctx.fillStyle='#e31820';
   ctx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.88)';
   ctx.shadowBlur=9;
   ctx.fillText('VICTORY',0,0);
   ctx.restore();
 }

 ctx.restore();
}

 // Higher on map = farther away = rendered first.

function recoil(enemy,done,dir=1,strong=false){
 const token=ACTIVE_ACTION_TOKEN;
 startHitReaction(enemy.name,dir,strong);
 let ox=enemy.x,oy=enemy.y,dx=18*(dir||1),start=performance.now();
 function f(n){
  if(!actionTokenAlive(token))return;
  let state=ensureAnimState(),t=Math.min(1,(n-start)/130),off=Math.sin(t*Math.PI)*dx;
  state.positions[enemy.name]={x:ox+off,y:oy};
  if(t<1)return requestAnimationFrame(f);
  delete state.positions[enemy.name];
  try{done&&done()}catch(err){console.error('Recoil callback failed:',err);recoverAction('recoil callback')}
 }
 requestAnimationFrame(f)
}
function resolvePlayer(){

 let p=S.ready.ref,u=front(p),useJutsu=S.action==='jutsu',proxy=attackProxy(u);
 const supportJutsu=useJutsu&&isAllySupportJutsu(u);
 let targets=supportJutsu?[]:aliveEnemies().filter(e=>hits(p,proxy,e));
 p.gauge=0;S.phase='resolve';S.anim={positions:{}};const playerActionToken=beginActionToken();armActionWatchdog('player attack',playerActionToken);

 if(useJutsu&&u.chakra<u.jutsuCost){
  S.log=`${u.name} does not have enough chakra.`;S.action='normal';return finishAction()
 }

 if(supportJutsu){
  u.chakra-=u.jutsuCost;

  const allies=alivePairs().filter(pair=>front(pair).hp>0);
  if(!allies.length){
   S.log=`${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'}, but no allies can be healed.`;
   return finishAction();
  }

  const teamCenter={
   x:allies.reduce((sum,pair)=>sum+pair.x,0)/allies.length,
   y:allies.reduce((sum,pair)=>sum+pair.y,0)/allies.length-18
  };

  const from={x:p.x,y:p.y};
  S.log=`${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Revival Formula'} — restoring all active allies to full HP.`;

  return animateSenkuChemicalReaction(
   u.name,from,teamCenter,
   ()=>{
    allies.forEach(pair=>{
     const ally=front(pair);
     const healed=Math.max(0,ally.maxHp-ally.hp);
     ally.hp=ally.maxHp;

     addImpactFlash(pair.x,pair.y-18,'#64ff7d');
     if(healed>0){
      addFloat(pair.x,pair.y-40,`+${healed}`,'#74ff91');
     }else{
      addFloat(pair.x,pair.y-40,'FULL','#74ff91');
     }
    });
   },
   ()=>finishAction()
  );
 }

 if(!targets.length){
  S.log=`${u.name} committed the move but caught no target.`;return finishAction()
 }

 if(useJutsu){
  u.chakra-=u.jutsuCost;

  if(u.name==='Lebee'){
   S.log=`Lebee activated Meteor Jutsu — ${targets.length} target${targets.length===1?'':'s'} caught in the cosmic impact zone.`;
   const from={x:p.x,y:p.y};
   return animateLebeeMeteorJutsu(
    u.name,from,targets,
    ()=>{
     targets.forEach(enemy=>{
      if(enemy.hp<=0)return;
      enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-u.jutsuDamage);
      addImpactFlash(enemy.x,enemy.y-8,'#ff7ae6');
      addFloat(enemy.x,enemy.y-34,'-'+u.jutsuDamage,'#ff8be8');
      const dir=Math.sign(enemy.x-p.x)||1;
      if(enemy.hp<=0)handleEnemyKO(enemy,dir); else recoil(enemy,()=>{},dir,true);
     });
     checkVictoryKillshot();
    },
    ()=>finishAction()
   );
  }

  // Every Jutsu target gets:
  //   active fighter's Jutsu hit
  //   + normal attacks from OTHER allies whose normal range covers that target.
  let jutsuQueue=targets.map(enemy=>{
    let helpers=comboMembers(enemy,p).filter(helper=>helper!==p);
    return {enemy,helpers};
  });

  let anyHelpers=jutsuQueue.some(item=>item.helpers.length>0);
  S.log=anyHelpers
    ? `${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'} — normal combo partners are joining where their ranges overlap.`
    : `${u.name} activated ${u.jutsuName||'Jutsu'} — ${targets.length} target${targets.length===1?'':'s'} caught in the field.`;

  let targetIndex=0;

  function runJutsuTarget(){
   if(targetIndex>=jutsuQueue.length)return finishAction();

   let item=jutsuQueue[targetIndex++];
   let enemy=item.enemy;
   let helpers=item.helpers;

   // First: the active fighter performs the Jutsu hit.
   let dx=enemy.x-p.x,dy=enemy.y-p.y,len=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(dx,dy));
   let to={x:enemy.x-dx/len*((enemy.r||19)+27),y:enemy.y-dy/len*((enemy.r||19)+27)};
   let from={x:p.x,y:p.y};

   const runActiveJutsu=(u.name==='Sub-Zero')?animateFreezeBlast:animateLunge;
   const activeFrom=(u.name==='Sub-Zero')?from:from;
   const activeTarget=(u.name==='Sub-Zero')?enemy:to;
   runActiveJutsu(
    u.name,activeFrom,activeTarget,
    ()=>{
     enemy.hp=Math.max(0,enemy.hp-u.jutsuDamage);
     addImpactFlash(enemy.x,enemy.y-8,u.name==='Crimson'?'#ff405c':'#8cecff');
     addFloat(enemy.x,enemy.y-34,'-'+u.jutsuDamage,'#57e7ff');
     let jDir=Math.sign(enemy.x-p.x)||1;
     if(enemy.hp<=0)handleEnemyKO(enemy,jDir); else if(u.name!=='Sub-Zero')recoil(enemy,()=>{},jDir,true);
     if(u.name==='Sub-Zero')enemy.gauge=Math.max(0,(enemy.gauge||0)-35);
     checkVictoryKillshot();
    },
    ()=>{
     let helperIndex=0;

     function runHelper(){
      if(enemy.hp<=0 || helperIndex>=helpers.length)
       return setTimeout(runJutsuTarget,110);

      let hp=helpers[helperIndex++];
      let hu=front(hp);

const LEVEL1_PORTRAIT_MAP=new Image();
const ANUBIS_PORTRAIT_MAP=new Image();

function setBattleMap(kind){
 const isBoss=kind==='boss';
 const src=isBoss?window.BLAZING_ASSETS.maps.anubisBoss:window.BLAZING_ASSETS.maps.level1;
 const img=isBoss?ANUBIS_PORTRAIT_MAP:LEVEL1_PORTRAIT_MAP;

 // CSS fallback renders immediately even if Edge delays Image() decode.
 cvs.style.backgroundImage=`url("${src}")`;

 if(img.src!==src){
   img.src=src;
 }
 if(typeof img.decode==='function'){
   img.decode().catch(()=>{});
 }
}
setBattleMap('level');

function fresh(){
 return {
  time:0,phase:'charge',action:'normal',ready:null,readyStarted:0,_chargeSince:performance.now(),drag:false,dragOrigin:null,dragVisual:null,anim:null,floaters:[],victoryFX:null,jutsuDim:null,bossBattle:false,battleMode:'level',log:'Waiting for the first fighter to become ready.',

function freshBoss(){
 return {
  time:0,phase:'charge',action:'normal',ready:null,readyStarted:0,_chargeSince:performance.now(),drag:false,dragOrigin:null,dragVisual:null,anim:null,floaters:[],victoryFX:null,jutsuDim:null,
  log:'BOSS BATTLE — Anubis, Judge of Souls.',
  bossBattle:true,battleMode:'boss',

let S=fresh();

const front=p=>p.units[p.active], back=p=>p.units[1-p.active];
const pairAlive=p=>p.units.some(u=>u.hp>0);
const alivePairs=()=>S.pairs.filter(pairAlive);
const aliveEnemies=()=>S.enemies.filter(e=>e.hp>0);
const NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE={type:'circle',r:43};
const normalShape=()=>NORMAL_ATTACK_SHAPE;
const attackShape=u=>{
 // Strict separation:
 // NORMAL -> unit's canonical combat.basic_shape
 // JUTSU  -> unit's canonical combat.jutsu_shape
 if(S.action==='jutsu')return u.jutsuShape;
 return u.shape||normalShape();
};
function attackProxy(u){return {...u,shape:attackShape(u)}}

// Ability-tier lookup must live at battle scope because both the ground-field
// pass and the later enemy target-feedback pass use it.
function isUltimateAction(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   // canonicalUnit already resolves both IDs and display names through UNIT_NAME_TO_ID.
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return !!(ability?.ultimate===true || ability?.category==='ultimate' || ability?.tier==='ultimate');
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}

function isAllySupportJutsu(unit){
 if(!unit)return false;
 try{
   const ability=canonicalUnit(unit.name)?.abilities?.jutsu;
   return ability?.target==='all_allies' || ability?.effect==='heal_full';
 }catch(_){
   return false;
 }
}


function ensureFront(p){
 if(front(p).hp>0)return true;
 if(front(p).hp<=0 && koAnimating(front(p).name))return true;
 if(back(p).hp>0){p.active=1-p.active;return true}
 return false
}
function fxMap(){
 if(!S.anim)S.anim={positions:{}};
 if(!S.anim.unitFX)S.anim.unitFX={};
 return S.anim.unitFX;
}
function unitFX(name){
 return S.anim?.unitFX?.[name]||null;
}
function koAnimating(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration);
}
function renderWhileKO(name){
 let fx=unitFX(name);
 return !!(fx&&fx.kind==='ko'&&(performance.now()-fx.start)<fx.duration+90);
}
function startHitReaction(name,dir=1,strong=false){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'hit',start:performance.now(),duration:strong?230:180,dir:dir||1,strong:!!strong};
}
function startKOReaction(name,dir=1){
 fxMap()[name]={kind:'ko',start:performance.now(),duration:520,dir:dir||1};
}
function pairRenderable(p){
 return pairAlive(p)||renderWhileKO(front(p).name)||renderWhileKO(back(p).name);
}
function handleKO(p,defeatedName,dir=1){
 addFloat(p.x,p.y-58,'KO','#ff7272');
 startKOReaction(defeatedName,dir);
 p.gauge=0;
 if(back(p).hp>0){
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${back(p).name} is preparing to move to the front.`;
   setTimeout(()=>{
     if(front(p).name===defeatedName && back(p).hp>0){
       p.active=1-p.active;
       S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — ${front(p).name} automatically moved to the front.`;
     }
   },380);
 }else{
   S.log=`${defeatedName} was knocked out — that pair has been defeated.`;
 }
}
function handleEnemyKO(e,dir=1){
 addFloat(e.x,e.y-46,'KO','#ff7272');
 startKOReaction(e.name,dir);
 e.gauge=0;
 S.log=`${e.name} was defeated.`;
}

function local(p,u,e){let a=-(u.rotation||0),dx=e.x-p.x,dy=e.y-p.y;return{x:dx*Math.cos(a)-dy*Math.sin(a),y:dx*Math.sin(a)+dy*Math.cos(a)}}
function circleRect(q,r,hw,hh){let x=clamp(q.x,-hw,hw),y=clamp(q.y,-hh,hh);return Math.hypot(q.x-x,q.y-y)<=r}
function hits(p,u,e){let s=u.shape,q=local(p,u,e),er=e.r||20;
 if(s.type==='screen_rect')return e.x>=BATTLE_BOUNDS.left&&e.x<=BATTLE_BOUNDS.right&&e.y>=BATTLE_BOUNDS.top&&e.y<=BATTLE_BOUNDS.bottom;
 if(s.type==='circle')return d(p,e)<=s.r+er;
 if(s.type==='rect'){
   const rq={x:q.x-(s.offset_x||0),y:q.y-(s.offset_y||0)};
   return circleRect(rq,er,s.w/2,s.h/2);
 }
 if(s.type==='square')return circleRect(q,er,s.s/2,s.s/2);
 if(s.type==='cross')return circleRect(q,er,s.l/2,s.t/2)||circleRect(q,er,s.t/2,s.l/2);
 if(s.type==='donut'){let z=Math.hypot(q.x,q.y);return z+er>=s.i&&z-er<=s.o}
 if(s.type==='cone'){let z=Math.hypot(q.x,q.y),ang=Math.abs(Math.atan2(q.y,q.x)),pad=Math.asin(Math.min(1,er/Math.max(z,.01)));return q.x+er>=0&&z-er<=s.r&&ang<=s.a/2+pad}
 return false}

 // Neon-blue chakra pips sit on their own row below HP.
